09012:     function getVehicleTypeIdentifiers(input) {
09013:         if (!input) return [];
09014: 
09015:         const cached = mfVehicleTypeIdentifierCache.get(input);
09016:         if (cached) return cached;
09017: 
09018:         const row = input.closest?.('tr');
09019:         const typeCell = row?.querySelector?.(
09020:             '[vehicle_type_id], [data-vehicle-type-id]'
09021:         );
09022: 
09023:         const identifiers = [
09024:             input.getAttribute?.('vehicle_type'),
09025:             input.getAttribute?.('vehicle_type_id'),
09026:             input.getAttribute?.('data-vehicle-type'),
09027:             input.getAttribute?.('data-vehicle-type-id'),
09028:             row?.getAttribute?.('vehicle_type'),
09029:             row?.getAttribute?.('vehicle_type_id'),
09030:             row?.getAttribute?.('data-vehicle-type'),
09031:             row?.getAttribute?.('data-vehicle-type-id'),
09032:             typeCell?.getAttribute?.('vehicle_type_id'),
09033:             typeCell?.getAttribute?.('data-vehicle-type-id')
09034:         ]
09035:             .filter(value => value !== null && value !== undefined && String(value).trim() !== '')
09036:             .map(value => String(value).trim());
09037: 
09038:         mfVehicleTypeIdentifierCache.set(input, identifiers);
09039:         return identifiers;
09040:     }
09041: 
09042:     function isPoliceCarVehicleCheckbox(
09043:         input
09044:     ) {
09045:         if (!input) {
09046:             return false;
09047:         }
09048: 
09049:         const typeIdentifiers =
09050:             getVehicleTypeIdentifiers(
09051:                 input
09052:             );
09053: 
09054:         // MissionChief UK normal Police Car / IRV vehicle type.
09055:         if (
09056:             typeIdentifiers
09057:                 .includes(
09058:                     '8'
09059:                 )
09060:         ) {
09061:             return true;
09062:         }
09063: 
09064:         const values =
09065:             getCheckboxVehicleValues(
09066:                 input
09067:             );
09068: 
09069:         return values.some(value => {
09070:             const cleaned =
09071:                 normaliseVehicleText(
09072:                     value
09073:                 );
09074: 
09075:             return (
09076:                 cleaned ===
09077:                     'police car' ||
09078:                 cleaned ===
09079:                     'police cars' ||
09080:                 cleaned ===
09081:                     'incident response vehicle' ||
09082:                 cleaned ===
09083:                     'incident response vehicles' ||
09084:                 cleaned ===
09085:                     'irv'
09086:             );
09087:         });
09088:     }

07434:     // V10.6.67: The Personnel Tool registry can be contaminated when several

00926:     function registerToolLifecycleCleanup(cleanup) {
00927:         if (typeof cleanup === 'function') {
00928:             TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS.add(cleanup);
00929:         }
00930:         return cleanup;
00931:     }
00932: 
00933:     function runToolLifecycleCleanup() {
00934:         if (TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANED) return;
00935:         TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANED = true;
00936: 
00937:         STATE.stopped = true;
00938:         STATE.paused = false;
00939:         STATION_STATE.stopped = true;
00940:         STATION_STATE.paused = false;
00941:         PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped = true;
00942:         PERSONNEL_STATE.paused = false;
00943: 
00944:         flushPersonnelTrainingRegistry(true);
00945: 
00946:         if (PERSONNEL_STATE.activeController) {
00947:             try {
00948:                 PERSONNEL_STATE.activeController.abort();
00949:             } catch (_error) {}
00950:             PERSONNEL_STATE.activeController = null;
00951:         }
00952: 
00953:         for (const controller of STATION_STATE.activeControllers) {
00954:             try {
00955:                 controller.abort();
00956:             } catch (_error) {}
00957:         }
00958:         STATION_STATE.activeControllers.clear();
00959: 
00960:         for (const cleanup of [...TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS]) {
00961:             try {
00962:                 cleanup();
00963:             } catch (_error) {}
00964:         }
00965:         TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS.clear();
00966: 
00967:         for (const frameId of TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.values()) {
00968:             try {
00969:                 cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
00970:             } catch (_error) {}
00971:         }
00972:         TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.clear();
00973:         TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.clear();
00974:         PERSONNEL_STATION_LINK_BY_HREF.clear();
00975:         PERSONNEL_START_OPTION_BY_HREF.clear();
00976:         PERSONNEL_HIGHLIGHTED_STATION_LINKS.clear();
00977: 
00978:         STATION_STATE.activeIframe = null;
00979:         STATION_STATE.buildingCoordinateCache.clear();
00980:         STATION_STATE.buildingDataPromise = null;
00981:         PERSONNEL_STATE.reports = [];
00982:         PERSONNEL_STATE.lastStationReport = null;
00983:         PERSONNEL_STATE.currentReport = '';
00984:         PERSONNEL_STATE.currentStationReport = '';
00985:         PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE = null;
00986:         PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE = [];
00987:         PERSONNEL_SHORTFALL_CACHE_SIZE = -1;
00988:     }
00989: 
00990:     function getToolElementById(id) {
00991:         const key = String(id || '');
00992:         if (!key) return null;
00993: 
00994:         const cached = TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.get(key);
00995:         if (cached?.isConnected) return cached;
00996: 
00997:         const element = document.getElementById(key);
00998:         if (element) TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.set(key, element);
00999:         else TOOL_UI_ELEMENT_CACHE.delete(key);
01000:         return element;
01001:     }
01002: 
01003:     function clearBoundedToolLog(logBox) {
01004:         if (!logBox) return;
01005: 
01006:         const frameId = TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.get(logBox);
01007:         if (frameId != null) {
01008:             try {
01009:                 cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);
01010:             } catch (_error) {}
01011:             TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.delete(logBox);
01012:         }
01013: 
01014:         logBox.replaceChildren();
01015:     }
01016: 
01017:     function appendBoundedToolLog(logBox, line) {
01018:         if (!logBox || !line) return;
01019: 
01020:         logBox.appendChild(line);
01021: 
01022:         if (logBox.childElementCount > TOOL_LOG_MAX_LINES + TOOL_LOG_TRIM_BUFFER) {
01023:             const removeCount = logBox.childElementCount - TOOL_LOG_MAX_LINES;
01024:             for (let index = 0; index < removeCount; index++) {
01025:                 logBox.firstElementChild?.remove();
01026:             }
01027:         }
01028: 
01029:         if (!TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.has(logBox)) {
01030:             const frameId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
01031:                 TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.delete(logBox);
01032:                 if (logBox.isConnected) logBox.scrollTop = logBox.scrollHeight;
01033:             });
01034:             TOOL_LOG_SCROLL_FRAMES.set(logBox, frameId);
01035:         }
01036:     }
01037: 
01038:     function initWhenReady() {
01039:         let tries = 0;
01040:         const timer = setInterval(() => {
01041:             tries++;
01042: 
01043:             if (isStationOverviewScreen()) {
01044:                 clearInterval(timer);
01045:                 TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS.delete(clearInitTimer);
01046:                 init();
01047:             }
01048: 
01049:             if (tries >= 40) {
01050:                 clearInterval(timer);
01051:                 TOOL_LIFECYCLE_CLEANUPS.delete(clearInitTimer);
01052:             }
01053:         }, 500);
01054: 
01055:         const clearInitTimer = () => clearInterval(timer);
01056:         registerToolLifecycleCleanup(clearInitTimer);
01057:     }
01058:

01061:     }

01833:         const parsed = Number.parseInt(String(value ?? '').trim(), 10);
01834:         if (!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 0) return 0;
01835:         return Math.min(9999, parsed);

02126:     function populatePersonnelProfileSelect(preferredProfileId = '') {
02127:         const select = document.querySelector('#mc-personnel-profile');
02128:         if (!select) return;
02129: 
02130:         const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition();

03995:     function prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
03996:         registry,
03997:         now = Date.now(),
03998:         maximumVehicles = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
03999:     ) {
04000:         const sourceVehicles = registry?.vehicles && typeof registry.vehicles === 'object'
04001:             ? registry.vehicles
04002:             : {};
04003:         const cutoff = now - PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_AGE_MS;
04004:         const validEntries = Object.entries(sourceVehicles)
04005:             .filter(([vehicleId, entry]) => {
04006:                 const updatedAt = Number(entry?.updatedAt || 0);
04007:                 return Boolean(
04008:                     vehicleId
04009:                     && entry
04010:                     && typeof entry === 'object'
04011:                     && Number.isFinite(updatedAt)
04012:                     && updatedAt >= cutoff
04013:                 );
04014:             })
04015:             .sort((left, right) => Number(right[1]?.updatedAt || 0) - Number(left[1]?.updatedAt || 0));
04016: 
04017:         const retainedEntries = validEntries.slice(0, Math.max(0, Number(maximumVehicles) || 0));
04018:         const vehicles = Object.fromEntries(retainedEntries);
04019:         const originalCount = Object.keys(sourceVehicles).length;
04020:         const retainedCount = retainedEntries.length;
04021: 
04022:         registry.vehicles = vehicles;
04023: 
04024:         return {
04025:             originalCount,
04026:             retainedCount,
04027:             removedCount: Math.max(0, originalCount - retainedCount)
04028:         };
04029:     }
04030: 
04031:     function savePersonnelTrainingRegistry(registry) {
04032:         const now = Date.now();
04033:         const sourceVehicles = registry?.vehicles && typeof registry.vehicles === 'object'
04034:             ? registry.vehicles
04035:             : {};
04036:         const sourceCount = Object.keys(sourceVehicles).length;
04037:         const lastPrunedAt = Number(registry?.lastPrunedAt || 0);
04038:         const shouldPrune = sourceCount > PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
04039:             || !Number.isFinite(lastPrunedAt)
04040:             || now - lastPrunedAt >= PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS;
04041:         const primaryPrune = shouldPrune
04042:             ? prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
04043:                 registry,
04044:                 now,
04045:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_MAX_VEHICLES
04046:             )
04047:             : {
04048:                 originalCount: sourceCount,
04049:                 retainedCount: sourceCount,
04050:                 removedCount: 0
04051:             };
04052: 
04053:         if (shouldPrune) registry.lastPrunedAt = now;
04054: 
04055:         try {
04056:             localStorage.setItem(
04057:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY,
04058:                 JSON.stringify(registry)
04059:             );
04060: 
04061:             return {
04062:                 saved: true,
04063:                 pruned: primaryPrune.removedCount,
04064:                 retained: primaryPrune.retainedCount,
04065:                 quotaRetry: false
04066:             };
04067:         } catch (error) {
04068:             const isQuotaError = error?.name === 'QuotaExceededError'
04069:                 || error?.name === 'NS_ERROR_DOM_QUOTA_REACHED'
04070:                 || Number(error?.code) === 22
04071:                 || Number(error?.code) === 1014;
04072: 
04073:             if (!isQuotaError) throw error;
04074: 
04075:             const retryPrune = prunePersonnelTrainingRegistry(
04076:                 registry,
04077:                 now,
04078:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_QUOTA_RETRY_MAX_VEHICLES
04079:             );
04080:             registry.lastPrunedAt = now;
04081: 
04082:             localStorage.setItem(
04083:                 PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_STORAGE_KEY,
04084:                 JSON.stringify(registry)
04085:             );
04086: 
04087:             return {
04088:                 saved: true,
04089:                 pruned: Math.max(primaryPrune.removedCount, retryPrune.removedCount),
04090:                 retained: retryPrune.retainedCount,
04091:                 quotaRetry: true
04092:             };
04093:         }
04094:     }

04149:     function flushPersonnelTrainingRegistry(silent = false) {
04150:         if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER != null) {
04151:             clearTimeout(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER);
04152:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = null;
04153:         }
04154: 
04155:         if (!PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY || !PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE) {
04156:             return { saved: false, pruned: 0, retained: 0, quotaRetry: false };
04157:         }
04158: 
04159:         try {
04160:             const registrySave = savePersonnelTrainingRegistry(PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_CACHE);
04161:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = false;
04162: 
04163:             const pendingEvent = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT;
04164:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = null;
04165:             dispatchPersonnelTrainingRegistryUpdate(pendingEvent);
04166: 
04167:             if (!silent) {
04168:                 personnelDebug(
04169:                     `Shared training register saved: retained=${registrySave.retained} | pruned=${registrySave.pruned}${registrySave.quotaRetry ? ' | quota retry used' : ''}`
04170:                 );
04171:             }
04172: 
04173:             return registrySave;
04174:         } catch (error) {
04175:             if (!silent) {
04176:                 personnelLog(`Shared training register could not be saved: ${error?.message || error}`, 'error');
04177:             }
04178:             return { saved: false, pruned: 0, retained: 0, quotaRetry: false, error };
04179:         }
04180:     }

04182:     function schedulePersonnelTrainingRegistryFlush() {
04183:         if (PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER != null) return;
04184: 
04185:         PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = setTimeout(() => {
04186:             PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_TIMER = null;
04187:             flushPersonnelTrainingRegistry(false);
04188:         }, PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_FLUSH_DELAY_MS);
04189:     }

04191:     function countPersonnelTrainingCodes(
04192:         assignedPersonnel
04193:     ) {
04194:         const counts = {};
04195: 
04196:         (
04197:             Array.isArray(
04198:                 assignedPersonnel
04199:             )
04200:                 ? assignedPersonnel
04201:                 : []
04202:         ).forEach(person => {
04203:             const uniqueCodes =
04204:                 new Set(
04205:                     (
04206:                         Array.isArray(
04207:                             person?.trainingCodes
04208:                         )
04209:                             ? person.trainingCodes
04210:                             : []
04211:                     )
04212:                         .map(String)
04213:                         .filter(Boolean)
04214:                 );
04215: 
04216:             uniqueCodes.forEach(code => {
04217:                 counts[code] =
04218:                     Number(
04219:                         counts[code] ||
04220:                         0
04221:                     ) + 1;
04222:             });
04223:         });
04224: 
04225:         return counts;
04226:     }

04228:     function publishPersonnelVehicleTrainingRegistry({
04229:         station,
04230:         vehicles,
04231:         personnel,
04232:         source = 'scan'
04233:     }) {
04234:         const stationVehicles = Array.isArray(vehicles)
04235:             ? vehicles.filter(vehicle => vehicle?.vehicleId)
04236:             : [];
04237: 
04238:         if (!stationVehicles.length) return 0;
04239: 
04240:         const registry = readPersonnelTrainingRegistry();
04241:         const now = Date.now();
04242:         const currentVehicleIds = new Set(
04243:             stationVehicles.map(vehicle => String(vehicle.vehicleId))
04244:         );
04245:         const processedTypeIds = new Set(
04246:             stationVehicles.map(vehicle => String(vehicle.vehicleTypeId || ''))
04247:         );
04248: 
04249:         Object.entries(registry.vehicles).forEach(([vehicleId, entry]) => {
04250:             if (String(entry?.stationHref || '') !== String(station?.href || '')) return;
04251:             if (!processedTypeIds.has(String(entry?.vehicleTypeId || ''))) return;
04252:             if (!currentVehicleIds.has(String(vehicleId))) delete registry.vehicles[vehicleId];
04253:         });
04254: 
04255:         stationVehicles.forEach(vehicle => {
04256:             const assignedPersonnel = getPersonnelAssignedToVehicle(personnel, vehicle);
04257:             const vehicleId = String(vehicle.vehicleId);
04258: 
04259:             registry.vehicles[vehicleId] = {
04260:                 vehicleId,
04261:                 vehicleName: String(vehicle.name || ''),
04262:                 vehicleTypeId: String(vehicle.vehicleTypeId || ''),
04263:                 stationName: String(station?.displayName || station?.stationName || ''),
04264:                 stationHref: String(station?.href || ''),
04265:                 assignedPersonnelCount: assignedPersonnel.length,
04266:                 trainingCounts: countPersonnelTrainingCodes(assignedPersonnel),
04267:                 updatedAt: now,
04268:                 source: String(source)
04269:             };
04270:         });
04271: 
04272:         registry.schemaVersion = PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_SCHEMA_VERSION;
04273:         registry.sourceVersion = PERSONNEL_VERSION;
04274:         registry.updatedAt = now;
04275:         PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_DIRTY = true;
04276:         PERSONNEL_TRAINING_REGISTRY_PENDING_EVENT = {
04277:             stationName: station?.displayName || '',
04278:             vehicleCount: stationVehicles.length,
04279:             updatedAt: now
04280:         };
04281: 
04282:         const finalSnapshot = /final-verification|station-complete/i.test(String(source));
04283:         if (finalSnapshot) flushPersonnelTrainingRegistry(false);
04284:         else schedulePersonnelTrainingRegistryFlush();
04285: 
04286:         personnelDebug(
04287:             `Shared training register cache updated: ${stationVehicles.length} vehicle(s) at ${station?.displayName || 'station'} | source=${source}${finalSnapshot ? ' | flushed' : ' | write queued'}`
04288:         );
04289: 
04290:         return stationVehicles.length;
04291:     }
04292:     function getPersonnelAssignmentIndex(personnel) {
04293:         if (!Array.isArray(personnel)) {
04294:             return { byVehicleId: new Map(), byVehicleName: new Map() };
04295:         }
04296: 
04297:         const cached = PERSONNEL_ASSIGNMENT_INDEX_CACHE.get(personnel);
04298:         if (cached) return cached;
04299: 
04300:         const byVehicleId = new Map();
04301:         const byVehicleName = new Map();
04302: 
04303:         personnel.forEach(person => {
04304:             const vehicleId = String(person?.assignedVehicleId || '');
04305:             const vehicleName = normalizePersonnelVehicleName(person?.assignedVehicleName || '');
04306: 
04307:             if (vehicleId) {
04308:                 if (!byVehicleId.has(vehicleId)) byVehicleId.set(vehicleId, []);
04309:                 byVehicleId.get(vehicleId).push(person);
04310:             }
04311: 
04312:             if (vehicleName) {
04313:                 if (!byVehicleName.has(vehicleName)) byVehicleName.set(vehicleName, []);
04314:                 byVehicleName.get(vehicleName).push(person);
04315:             }
04316:         });
04317: 
04318:         const index = { byVehicleId, byVehicleName };
04319:         PERSONNEL_ASSIGNMENT_INDEX_CACHE.set(personnel, index);
04320:         return index;
04321:     }

06033:     function getPersonnelReportResult(report) {
06034:         if (report?.error) return 'ERROR';
06035:

06037:             return report?.stationTargetMet

06040:         }
06041:

06043:         if (Number(report?.assignmentShortfall || 0) > 0) return 'SHORTFALL';
06044:         return 'COMPLETE';
06045:     }

06181:     function buildPersonnelOverallStationBlock(report, isAssign) {
06182:         if (!report) return '';
06183:

06185:         if (cached && cached.isAssign === isAssign) return cached.text;

06207:                             `  Additional units required: ${Number(report.stationTargetShortfallUnits || 0)}`
06208:                         ]
06209:                         : []
06210:                 ),
06211:                 `  Units fully covered: ${Number(report.fulfilledUnits || 0)}`,
06212:                 `  Required qualified positions: ${Number(report.required || 0)}`,
06213:                 `  Qualified personnel visible: ${Number(report.trained || 0)}`,
06214:                 `  Available qualified before: ${Number(report.availableAtScan || 0)}`,
06215:                 `  Available qualified after: ${Number(report.availableAtEnd || 0)}`,
06216:                 `  Qualified positions before: ${getPersonnelReportInitialFilled(report)}/${Number(report.required || 0)}`,
06217:                 `  ${isAssign ? 'Qualified positions after' : 'Projected qualified positions'}: ${getPersonnelReportFinalFilled(report)}/${Number(report.required || 0)}`,
06218:                 `  Qualification shortfall estimate: ${Number(report.trainingShortfall || 0)}`,
06219:                 `  Missing training by qualification: ${formatPoliceTrainingGapSummary(report.missingTrainingSummary)}`,
06220:                 `  ${isAssign ? 'Personnel assigned' : 'Assignments planned'}: ${isAssign ? Number(report.assigned || 0) : Number(report.planned || 0)}`,
06221:                 `  Unfilled required positions after action: ${Number(report.assignmentShortfall || 0)}`
06222:             );
06223: 
06224:             if (report.verificationError) lines.push(`  Verification warning: ${report.verificationError}`);

06226:             if (Array.isArray(report.rules) && report.rules.length > 1) {
06227:                 report.rules.forEach(rule => {
06228:                     lines.push(
06229:                         `    Rule: ${rule.label} | vehicles ${rule.vehicleCount} | target ${rule.target} | required ${rule.required} | trained ${rule.trained} | shortfall ${rule.trainingShortfall} | missing ${formatPoliceTrainingGapSummary(rule.trainingGaps)}`
06230:                     );
06231:                 });
06232:             }
06233:

06238:                     : '';
06239:                 const seatText = vehicle.occupiedSeatBlock
06240:                     ? ` | ${vehicle.occupiedSeatBlock} required position(s) blocked by existing occupants`
06241:                     : '';

06246:                 const beforeQualified = Number(vehicle.beforeQualified ?? vehicle.beforeCritical ?? 0);

06256:                 );
